# THE HARD SAYING — Knowledge Ledger (who knows what, when, how)

> The Knowledge Auditor's ledger. For every character: what they know about the others and about key facts, and **where
> and how** they learned it (episode, scene id, branch condition). A character may only reference what is recorded here or
> is clearly learnable on the page in the current scene.
>
> **Hidden prior knowledge:** some characters arrive at the pilot already knowing things the player doesn't. Those facts
> are hidden canon and are recorded in `bible.md` §13 ("Hidden prior knowledge at the pilot"). The Knowledge Auditor must
> read §13 alongside this file. Do not copy those facts here until the story reveals them on the page; when it does, log
> them below with the scene id.
>
> Format for each entry: `- [S1E0X sNN | condition] <fact> — <how learned>`

---

## Starting state (before S1E01, as far as the page is concerned)

Nobody in the core cast has met anybody else on-page. Specifically:

### Jack Costigan
- Knows his own family and history (Youngstown; Frank's layoff; Danny; Nonna; Gina; Rosie).
- Knows **of** Prof. Adrian Hale: has read *The Consolations of Disbelief* (2006); wrote Hale a nine-page letter in 2008;
  received a postcard in reply, signed "A.H.", which he uses as a bookmark. Has never seen Hale except in a book-jacket
  photo.
- Knows he is enrolled in PHIL 9H (Hale) and PHIL 1. Does not know who else is.
- Knows nothing about Lena, Theo, Laney, Margot, Kevin, Osei, Julian, Priya, Rafi, Marisol Point, the Lighthouse, or
  anyone's history at Marisol.
- Knows his roommate's name from a housing email: "Theodore Vance, Los Angeles." Nothing else.

### Lena Broussard
- Knows her own family and history.
- Knows **of** Hale (his book). Knows she is enrolled in PHIL 9H and PHIL 1.
- Knows nothing about Jack, Theo, Margot, Kevin (see §13), Julian (see §13). Knows Laney from housing emails and move-in
  (arrived Friday/Saturday; they've met in person by the pilot's evening).
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Theo Vance
- Knows his roommate's name from a housing email: "John Costigan, Youngstown, OH." Nothing else.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Laney Price
- Knows Lena (roommate; met at move-in). Knows Sloane Kittredge (Pi Lam rush contact; met over the summer at a Dallas
  recruitment tea). Knows no one else.

### Margot Lindqvist
- Knows no one. Lives alone in 305.

### Kevin Tran
- Knows Doug Pruitt (roommate; met at move-in Friday). Knows Joey Abruzzo (Newman Facebook group, met Friday).
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Fr. Gabriel Osei
- Knows his parishioners, Mrs. Fitch, the missionaries, Peter and Kenzie.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Prof. Adrian Hale
- Knows the names on his PHIL 9H roster. Remembers some applicants' letters (Jack's; others). Has never seen the faces of
  the incoming freshmen.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Julian Park-Ellis
- Knows none of the freshmen.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Priya Raman
- Knows her residents' names from the roster (4th floor: Jack, Theo, Kevin, Doug…). Knows nobody personally yet.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Rafi Ochoa
- Knows Marisol (local). Knows none of the freshmen.
- *(Further prior knowledge: bible §13.)*

### Frank Costigan / the Costigans
- Know nothing about anyone at Marisol.

## Name-introduction rules for S1E01 (CONFIRMED against the final episode)
- Jack learns "Lena"/"Magdalena Broussard" only when Laney shouts it (s10).
- Lena learns "Jack" when he tells her (s10); "Costigan" only when Tyler shouts it (s16).
- Jack learns "Margot" at s07c (front-yard route) or s13; "Lindqvist" at s13.
- Jack learns "Kevin Tran" at s05 (Kevin says it); "Joey Abruzzo" at s07a (kitchen) or s14 (else); "Rafi" at s07a only;
  "Delaney Price" at s07b (living) or s20 (all); "Sloane" at s11; "Mrs. Fitch" at s14 (no first name); "Tyler Brandt" at
  s14; "Gabriel Osei" at s14; "Peter Haddad"/"Kenzie Rourke" at s15 (Kevin); "Gloria" at s20 (name tag).
- Jack learns "Wes" only at s22.
- Julian and the man at the end of the driveway are never named in S1E01.

- **Mid-episode revision, `[s2e8_noon=="candor"]` only.** **Skye** now knows, from Jack at s02: Osei's fall counsel as Jack reported it (stop seeing her or stop what happens; change the hour; keep her in the daylight); that "the bad doesn't cancel out the good" is Jack's own sentence, not Osei's; that Jack means to tell Osei he spoke for him; that Jack counts Friday as both good and confessable, and does not put her in either "column"; she has not learned what Osei answered (seal; Jack may tell her himself later). **Osei** now knows (under the seal, s15): that Jack spoke for him to Skye on Monday and what he said; Osei stands by the good, corrects the hour (daylight kept at noon with the door shut), and warns that making her "the answer" would make her an occasion. **Jack** carries Osei's correction (s15a journal, s17 reading). LIVE RISK: Skye's "thing I put back" came up again "closer" on this path; it is spent at s16h/s16p (she names the second time as a would-be *thank you*); do not have her claim she never wavered.
- **Mid-episode revision 2, `[s2e8_d3=="real"]` only (s13r/s13r2).** **Lena now knows (from Jack, before Hale's table):** that Jack claims she "as good as told" him to go ahead, citing the Friday call (S2E03 s09: seeing him Monday, "fix it," "Okay. Then call me after. Eat something first.") and the birthday-dinner picture as proof it "got fixed"; that he read "Will you be around after?" as her wanting him on call to hear how dinner went; that he thinks she organizes people onto clipboards ([d4 personal]); that he quotes "you can stop" back at her; that he says she can't forgive a status. **She believes he is lying** — rewriting a call he understood perfectly, to win before a table. She did NOT say what she meant (the Julian breakup intent stays hers; "and I told you —" was stopped). **Jack now believes she is lying** — denying words she said, to win; her silence when he dared her ([d4 press]) is proof to him. **Both now think the other is a liar; it hardens them both.** Jack learned nothing new about her intent; he reads "I asked you one thing… you had a *thing*" as her wanting a friend on call after a date. **The table** (Hale, Theo, Laney, Caleb, Yusuf, Evan) heard Jack's version of the Friday call and the "liar" exchange; phones in the bowl, nothing left on a phone. **Laney** now holds Jack's misreading of the Friday call (she knows Lena's side); she looked at Jack long at the door; what she does with it is a future decision, off-page by default. **Theo** heard it all; "Don't ever make me watch that again." **Hale** stopped it; he does not diagnose it ("whatever was said on whose telephone… is your affair and hers"). **Osei** (seal) heard only "a friend," a liar charge, the cruelty; his "I would be less sure" is counsel, not knowledge. **Skye** learns nothing of the fight (she sees it on him; asks nothing).
- **LIVE RISK — the slow fuse (`[s2e8_d3=="real"]`).** Neither learns the truth soon. Lena must not explain the call before the NOLA layers (nola_ledger #1/#2/#14/#17); Jack must not reread the call in light of anything before Mardi Gras. On this path every later scene should carry the hardening: each thinks the other lied to their face before witnesses. The public "liar" is a debt both owe; the eventual answer must undo it explicitly, from both sides. Lena does NOT joke about Skye (her "Is that one hers?" [d4 wit] and "tagged in her bed" are attacks on the publishing, not jokes). Lena's "Do you still go up?" is a question, not knowledge (she does not know he didn't receive on Sunday). Jack must never cite the porch, the song or "a week" from this fight; none were said. Julian is not named on the page. If Laney reports Jack's version to Lena, Lena disbelieves it (consistent with "So was I").

#### LIVE RISKS carried out of S2E10
- **The Nola trip is set but its mechanics are fragile:** Friday 5 a.m., the Odyssey, Dupre Street attic, seven seats, sixty-one dollars a head, Laney's labeled bags; Kevin drives the first shift, Jack West Texas. Gloria's yes is in doubt; Hale's essay terms are unverified. E11 must keep both in doubt, not resolve them by gloss.
- **Skye's Vancouver pilot (two weeks, flying Friday morning)** collides with the trip window; she tipped it herself ("maybe it'll be good for you guys"), and Jack said yes on every path. Do not let her explain the tip.
- **The call with Danny ended with a question Skye never heard;** she must never learn it. Montecito facts are now Jack's.
- **`confession_owed` is 0.** The S2-e verge was confessed on every path ([Restraint] sets `promised_this_week`); a new rung would need a new owed count. Skye's "when" and "the good thing, with the lights off" (carried from E09) are still hers.
- **`resolve.lena` is 3 and pinned** (set on s03, s08 and s10); no dangerous night is open.
- **Meeting #3:** whiteboard with a car and six circles; cover "Book club. *Dubliners*." / "Science."; Jack heard "she'd *decided*, Theo" with no inference; two legal pads ("wedding seating chart"); Theo asked about the week of the seventh. Do not let Jack or Skye decode it.
- **Hale's dog is now canon in two mouths** (Hale to Jack; Hale to Osei); Osei's route is "Adrian tells me…", outside the seal. The small black book stays unglossed.
- **Lena's six minutes behind Hale's door and the flats stopping in the aisle at Candlemas** are unglossed; do not explain either.
- **Danny's card:** it went inside Gina's envelope Friday (two cards); the envelope had not arrived by Tuesday; Skye's letter had. Danny's number is A555218; Trumbull takes books only from a seller (Skye bought from Tidewater Books).
- **Branch-dependent:** `s2e10_mon` (Fishbowl: Kevin sees TRUMBULL, Margot's six versions; 412: Theo's study group), `s2e10_ma` (sincerity: Ma knows the song was for Danny), `s2e10_later` (candor: Skye knows "good name for a wife"), `s2e10_candle` (whose candle sits on the desk), `s2e10_verge` (Candor / Reckless / Sincerity / Restraint), `s2e10_amend`, `s2e10_after` (porch / lot), `reliquary_opened`.
- **Danny's birthday was Tuesday Feb 2** (turned twenty-three); Glenwood "next Christmas" is the visit line, and the Mary Catherine line is gated on `danny_laughed_name`.

#### LIVE RISKS carried out of S2E11
- **Skye's reasons (Sunday reason):** she gave it herself in s09b; Jack reports it under the seal in s10; Jack's own answer in s09c is "Thirty hours. In a van, with that in it." Do not have Skye learn Jack told Osei.
- **Danny's question still hidden from Skye:** the inherited Skye card base (S2E09) says Jack told her "Nonna's question and Danny's." That conflicts with this risk; the Head Writer left it untouched. The Knowledge Auditor must reconcile it upstream before E12 uses it. She still never learns Danny's question.
- **The pool house and WHO DAT · 3 DAYS:** Jack made no connection ("Don't go in the pool house" / "Sandbags."; Julian isn't coming). Do not let Jack decode it, and keep it off Skye. Together with "she'd *decided*, Theo", the folded legal-pad page and Kevin's unread second sheet in the glovebox, these are Theo/Laney/Lena threads running through the drive.
- **The mailed *Moviegoer*:** Jack owns Lena's copy with her pencil in it and has read it; Hale lent his annotated copy and doesn't know. Lena does not know Jack kept it. Do not resolve it by gloss.
- **Lena heard Jack's "Why would I care"** on every Julian branch; she saw the blessing and goodbye kiss (and, on `hill`, the arrival together); she knows nothing of the darkroom or S2-f.
- **`confession_owed` is 0 at s12.** S2-f was confessed under the seal; the outcome is `s2e11_sorry`, with `promised_till_back` (Restraint) and `absolution_withheld` (Reckless) as live consequences. Skye's `promised_skye_truth` is owed.
- **Scott Brown's truck:** "a GMC" (GMC Canyon, 2010 campaign); the Researcher should still sign off.
- **Branch-dependent:** `s2e11_evening` (playa: passport, *Port Alder*, flannel; tsix: Laney's bags, Theo's January probe), `s2e11_door`, `s2e11_yes` (Restraint "wait" and Reckless medal paths differ), `s2e11_truth`, `s2e11_hill` (hill / walk), `s2e11_sorry`, `s2e11_goodbye`, `s2e11_julian`, `s2e11_smoke` (`reliquary_opened`, `jack_cigs` on Reckless), `s2e11_print`, `s2e11_frank`.
- **The Nola trip is now under way:** left Friday on Laney's five; the van is on the 101 at 6:10 and at the Rincon at 6:48. Gloria's yes (said go) and Hale's terms (Go; four thousand words) are resolved to "go" but nothing past that is shown.

### S2E12
> Final revision (see continuity.md S2E12). Branch tags are `[scene | condition]`. Canon is Fri Feb 5 – Sat Feb 6, 2010 (tag Sat 11:50 PM, Dupre Street back steps). In the van (s01–s19) everything said aloud is heard by all six unless the entry says otherwise.

#### Jack
- **Sees or hears from Skye (phone):** [s04] two texts from Burnaby (light rain, 8 degrees; forecast rain tomorrow). [s06] her call from Burnaby at Picacho: she was met at the bottom of the escalator by a man in a fleece with a sign reading CASSIE; Wardrobe went through her bags, found his flannel and kept it (Cassie wears it in the porch scene, over the cardigan, sleeves down over her hands); Corinne on the elbows; the Labrador geese; six a.m. call; "Bye, Lake Erie" (the oil stain at the pump), after "Love you" [`s2e12_call=="sincerity"`], "Cassie can keep the shirt" [wit], or "I miss you. It's been twelve hours." / "Twelve and a quarter." [candor]. [s23/s23a/s23s] "You were in my single today": Cassie's porch, take nine, he was at her left shoulder in the flannel; the director said "The shirt's reading"; she told him it was method and not whose it was; "Call me at the kickoff" (she holds him to it on `s2e11_goodbye=="candor"`); "I miss you."
- **Learns about the van and the people in it:** [s02] Laney's lock-in and horse bite, Margot's 211 pages of field notes and the sorority minutes; in the visor mirror Laney is looking at Theo. [s05] Margot's eight versions of Wednesday, the eighth by Nokia: Julian heard and said nothing. [s08] Laney made Lena recite the January call twice at the mailboxes; Theo made Jack recite it once (Jack: "I said it word for word to one person, once, and that was Theo."); Kevin: "I said leave it alone. In January."; Jack concludes Lena lied to Laney so Laney would hate him, and Lena concludes the reverse. [s12] Theo and Laney at the ice machine: "It was a mistake." / "The whole thing." Jack says "Yeah." and learns nothing else; he sees Laney break a pill from an orange bottle in her train case and Theo's hand on her wrist ("It's a prescription, Theodore."); no inference. Kevin on the phone with Clare (CALL CLARE (TEXAS)), the Our Lady of La Vang. [s13] Kevin's second sheet from the glovebox, read in full by Jack and Lena: the rotation, "LANEY — NO. (UNANIMOUS.)" and in the margin "RESPECTFULLY — K."; Jack asks "Respectfully what?" and gets "The chart is the chart."
- **Learns from Lena (direct):** [s06a] "You always did read a margin well." [s07] she kept the receipts: "I'm talking about the *weather*… fourteen hundred miles"; she read the caption ("We agree on each other. *Not just friends from school.*" and, with `jack_profile_taqueria`, "happy looks good on you"). [s08] Kenzie Rourke told her she is on her side; Doug Pruitt's jam-jar story. [s09] she read *The Moviegoer* on the seawall at sixteen; Binx lives in Gentilly; her veil is black lace, bought with her own money, with no picture of her in it [Riposte]. [s10] her list for Skye ("the good girl for money, the honest girl for free"), Kenzie Rourke's side-door line, "Magdalena is done being gracious." [s14] "Quarter tank." [s15] Brees's 70.62 (since September); her father has been sick to his stomach since the Vikings, "since the *kick*", heard the overtime from the porch and lay on the front-room floor with a pillow under his head, wore a Schwegmann's bag to the Superdome in 1980 and prays "Lord, I wore the bag for You… You owe me one" out loud at the eight o'clock; the attic (a box fan from 1994 with "loud and louder", Remy's mattress and Tiger poster; "Don't move the poster"; Remy's fist behind it); Mama is making groceries Monday ("y'all are going to get drafted"); Mémère is 88 and sleeps in the front room "so she can see the stairs". [s15 | `s2e12_talk=="sincerity"`] "Baton Rouge. The big bridge. That's where you look at it." [s15 | `=="candor"`] what she eats first (Mémère's gumbo out of the pot, Daddy's boil, a cold Barq's from the bottle, a dressed shrimp po'boy on Leidenheimer's bread, a king cake she won't tell Laney about); [s15 | `=="restraint"`] the house after the water: her father and his brothers took it to the studs and put it back by hand, two years of Saturdays, she held the flashlight at fourteen, cypress under the front-room linoleum, "Not one of them shuts right." [s15a] her head on his shoulder for forty miles; at Fort Stockton neither of them mentions it. [s18v] "Is it any good? His?", and her own reply "Mine's better." [wit]. [s19] "I asked Kevin for the way-back so I wouldn't have to look at you for thirty hours… Turns out the way-back's where you see the back of everybody's head." [s19 | `candor`] she says nothing; one knock of her knuckle on his headrest. [s20] he carries her in; Celeste says she doesn't sleep in cars. #### LIVE RISKS carried out of S2E12
- **January call, now public in the van.** Margot and Kevin heard it whole; Theo and Laney already had it (Laney made Lena recite it twice, Theo made Jack recite it). Jack and Lena each believe the other lied to a best friend; neither has said what they told. Do not resolve it by gloss. Kevin's "I said leave it alone. In January." is unexplained.
- **Theo and Laney.** "It was a mistake. The whole thing." with Jack's "Yeah." is the only on-page statement; Laney's half-pill and the wrist hold are unexplained; both watched Jack and Lena from two windows (s24a). The pool house stays unconnected to the hill: on `heard_debris_flow` Jack knows the hill came down, the pool is a pond, and that Theo told his mother twice not to go in; on [candor] he gets "It's full of mud." Jack draws no inference and does not tell anyone. Theo's mother is at Bunny's on the phone and at the Huntington to Jack: a gloss, not a lie to resolve.
- **The mailed *Moviegoer*.** Resolved by degrees: on every s18v branch Lena has Jack judging her copy against Hale's; on `said_read_twice` she knows he kept and re-read it. Hale still does not know. Nothing past that is on the page (not what she wrote, not the passage).
- **The swing print** (NOT FOR POSTING) is still face-down in Hale's book on Jack's person; Lena has not seen its face; Skye has not been told where it lives. The Cabazon postcard for Danny (`danny_postcard`) is flat against the inside cover.
- **Skye's reasons and `promised_skye_truth`** are unchanged (Sunday reason: she gave it herself in S2E11 s09b; Jack reported it to Osei under the seal; Skye must not learn he told). The truth promise is still owed and was not touched this episode. Danny's question stays hidden from Skye (carried). Skye does not know about the fight or Lena's words.
- **Kickoff.** Skye holds Jack to the kickoff call (on `s2e11_goodbye=="candor"` she quotes his promise; otherwise "Call me at the kickoff"). Lena knows the promise (s10: "Who calls at a *kickoff*?").
- **`confession_owed` is 0**; the Sunday reading of Mark 6 (every morning till he's home) is owed again Sunday (Saturday's was done in Margot's Gideon, KJV; the book is Margot's). `jack_cigs` rises only on [Reckless]; the tin holds eleven.
- **The Dupre Street rules:** boys in the attic (Jack, Kevin, Theo), girls downstairs with Magdalena; Mémère sleeps in the front room where she can see the stairs. Josie knows "my girlfriend, in Canada" (`josie_asked`, steps branch only). Celeste knows Jack carried Lena (and, on [candor], that he knew he had).
- **Clocks.** WHO DAT is at 1 DAYS (s18), zero on Sunday; the dash clock runs an hour behind Central after the Hudspeth/Culberson line; the drive took 35 hours 40 minutes (Kevin's figure; Theo said thirty, "approximately").
- **Branch-dependent:** `s2e12_call`, `s2e12_margin`, `s2e12_d1`–`d4`, `s2e12_sa` (theo: s18t, the hill; van: s18v, the copy), `s2e12_talk`, `s2e12_basin`, `s2e12_celeste`, `s2e12_skyecall` (steps: s23s, Josie; attic: s23a, Kevin and Theo), `s2e12_goodnight`, `s2e12_line`.

- **Tony and the sign.** Tony knows Jack's nonna says it about sons (no name) only on the real save; Tony keeps the sign folded in his shirt pocket.
- **Lena's "— and —".** Unspoken after Hale (`hale_said_magdalena`). Do not fill it by narration.
- **Luc's file.** Luc wrote the 2006 gas death as "accidental"; Jack's scar story to Luc is one of fight / nun / husband and Luc knows only that one.
- **The ring.** Back with Mémère since November, in the cedar chest in Breaux Bridge; Jack and Lena both heard Josie say so.
- **Hale.** Knows both replies came from the same city; four thousand words due the Monday they return.
- **Carried from S2E13 and untouched:** Lena's "Since September." (no answer); Theo's eyes at S2E13 s12a; the January call, Kevin's "I said leave it alone. In January."; Theo and Laney's "mistake"; the swing print and the Cabazon postcard; `promised_skye_truth`; the pool house/hill; `confession_owed`.

### S2E15
> Final revision (see continuity.md S2E15). Branch tags are `[scene | condition]`. Canon is Thu Feb 11 – Sun Feb 14, 2010 (tag Sun 11:30 PM, the attic). Everything said aloud in a room is heard by the people in it unless the entry says otherwise. None of the hidden canon (ledgers, outlines, bible) appears on the page, in keys, cues or nextTime; the file has no journal.

#### Jack
- **Learns about the Broussards:** [s02] Mémère is anointed on the Lourdes feast; the grotto has red vigil lights; Lena gives Bernadette's line word for word. [s02a] Fr. Hébert hears confessions Saturday 3–4 (the vestibule card); Hébert calls him "Le garçon de l'Ohio". [s03g] Miss Dot Pellerin (next door to the market) has known Antoine thirty years; Mr. Dupuy (Lafitte), Miss Earline, Mr. Alcide; Broussard's Seafood is a mile away across Canal. [s03c] Kevin's two-dollar bills from Huong; "You mean it." / "Good. People should get to." [s04] Tammy's Friday flight is canceled by the Dallas snow (room on St. Charles); Laney's decree; Celeste's list on the back of the light bill. [s06] Celeste mailed Frank's paper. [s07] Destiny Thibodeaux's valentine; Jude asks him. [s07a] Celeste's list adds "candles — 19" (Jack and Lena told "Laney's not to see"). [s08a] Remy's Valentine's-movie idea is vetoed by Theo and Laney. [s09] Celeste's Grandmère's vase; Mémère: "le chanteur"; Margot's field note. [s11] Luc is on the NOPD detail; Tom and Gayle Benson are grand marshals. [s12] Bà Nội and Mémère speak French (both taught by nuns in Hanoi); Gordon called Laney from a 403 number, four minutes; Mémère: "Bonne fête, la rousse." [s13] Mémère's verdict at the gate by duel score. [s14/s14a] Vy Phạm (16); Mr. Phạm Văn Lộc and Kevin's father on Pulau Bidong in 1979 ("half a fish"); the aunties' "next year you two give… yet"; Margot seated as Kevin's girlfriend. [s17] Celeste's doberge; Tony's rule ("you're ours"); Laney cries at the table (first time since Kaylee). [s18] Lena's "Thank you for coming." [s18a] Kevin turns 19 tomorrow, "the first morning of the Goat" (`kevin_birthday_known`).
- **Learns from Lena:** [s02] Bernadette's line. [s08a] nothing that implies she heard "Love you" aloud (see Live risks; she keeps her eyes on the screen). [s15] she is awake with her head on his shoulder and answers "…What did I say?". [s18] she asks nothing about his birthday; she learns it is March 19.
- **Learns from the others:** [s06 | Candor] Tony answers about Frank (laid off from Lordstown): "tell him come down here." [s07a] Josie: "Mémère asked me who's in Canada." [s09] Josie reads "Canada" on his face. [s10a/s10b] Hébert's absolution and penance (a decade by her Christian name). [s14a] Margot says "cultural observer" to Jack only. [s15a] Frank's text: OSU 72, Illinois 53. [s16a] Skye sends a picture of the gift (not a rung).
- **Tells others:** to Mémère (s02): [`s2e15_lourdes`] his candle is for his brother / "a girl in Canada" (`memere_knows_canada` on Candor) / the whole house. To Celeste (s10, owed path): "Holy Rosary" (Lena hears it). To Hébert (s10a): a girlfriend in Canada and the picture opened at 2 AM; [candor] that he kept it; [sincerity] her name Skye, an actress, Vancouver, "that he loves her". To Kevin (s03c): the gift is for Skye (a Valentine). To Skye: (s01) the dawn text per `s2e15_dawn`; (s08) from the back steps, "Love you" [sincerity, `said_love_fri`] under the open window above Lena; (s16) she has the gift; "Mary Kate" under his breath, too low to carry to the curb [Sincerity]; [pew path] "It was me" (he did not go up); "I'm on St. Charles, waiting on a king" is hers to him; "Back steps." To Lena (s15): [`s2e15_try`] "What you said on Esplanade." and then nothing / "you wanted me here" / "your mother would kill you". To Jude (s07): [sincerity] check yes / [candor] "Tell her the truth before the parade." / [wit] four years and California. To Tony (s06): [`s2e15_ladder`]. To Phạm's grandmother (s14a): his wish by `s2e15_tet`.
- **Does not know / draws no inference:** what Lena said on Esplanade beyond what he told her (he does not recover it); whether Lena heard "Love you" on the steps beyond her reaction; Lena's "— and —"; Theo's eyes at S2E13 s12a; who the penance prayer was said for beyond Hébert's instruction.
- **Does:** [s03b] buys *Mystery and Manners* (FSG 1969, $18) and writes "Mary Kate" on the flyleaf; [s03c] FedEx International Priority to her Burnaby hotel, $66.40, SUNDAY on the box; [s03g] white violets in a pot, $31; [s06] drills the gold ladder box; [s13] prays the decade by her Christian name; [s16a] catches the Bacchus medallion and gives it to Lena, Laney or Josie.

#### Lena
- **Hears on every path:** [s03g | violets] Miss Dot: "violets. In February." [s04] Laney's decree. [s07a] "Laney's not to see." [s08a] the broadcast with the window up. [s09] Julian's roses and Celeste's vase. [s11] Laney's "NO hearts". [s12] Bà Nội and Mémère in French. [s13] the duel and Mémère's verdict. [s14a] the aunties' "next year you two give… yet". [s17] the cake and Laney's cry. [s18] Jack's birthday, March 19.

[s10, owed path] "Holy Rosary" (with Celeste). [s11] her arms round his waist. [s15] his try about Esplanade and the retreat she hears. [s18] "Not Youngstown, not Ohio." [s07 | Candor] she overhears "tell her the truth before the parade." [s11] her s11 line about the confession has a route only on the owed path (she heard "Holy Rosary"); [pew path] she sees him stay on the pew at Communion.
- **Sees or hears herself:** [s02] Mémère's anointing. [s13] Jack stays or goes up (`stayed_pew_feb14` / `went_up_feb14`). [s16a] the medallion if Jack throws to her (`throw_to_lena`; the hand-finding only in this branch). [s18] her own try, "Thank you for coming."

#### Skye
- **From Jack (phone/text):** [s01] the dawn text per `s2e15_dawn`. [s08] the torch call: "jealous of a director"; Skye now knows he is on the back steps; [sincerity] "Love you" (`said_love_fri`). [s16] she has the gift and says "Nobody sends me the first thing"; [book] *Mystery and Manners* with "Mary Kate" on the flyleaf and his inscription per `s2e15_card`; [violets] white violets from Miss Dot's, "told me to come"; [pew path] "It was me" (he did not go up); [Sincerity] nothing of "Mary Kate" is audible. [s18a] "clear. 9".

#### LIVE RISKS carried out of S2E15
- **`said_love_fri`.** Lena hears "Love you" through the open window above her (s08a shows it). Do not have her surprised in E16; also do not have her say it aloud unless a scene earns it.
- **"Mary Kate".** Jack writes it on the flyleaf and, on [Sincerity] at s16, says it under his breath, too low to carry. Skye has the book; she may have read the flyleaf. No one on the curb heard the name aloud. The name was the father's gift, not Debbie's.
- **Lena and the gift.** Lena knows it was a Valentine (s05) and, on violets, that they are white; she does not know about "Mary Kate" or the flyleaf.
- **Confession branches.** Owed path: absolved, `confession_owed` 0, Celeste and Lena heard "Holy Rosary"; Hébert knows Skye's name only on [Sincerity]; the "kept it" only on [Candor]. Not-yet path: `confession_waits`, `stayed_pew_feb14`; Lena sees him stay on the pew; Skye is told at s16 ("It was me").
- **`memere_knows_canada`.** Only on [Candor] at the grotto; Josie also learns "Canada" at s07a/s09.
- **Kevin's loan.** $12, "I'll invoice you"; Kevin turns 19 tomorrow (Monday Feb 15), which Jack alone learns at s18a.
- **Skye's "clear. 9".** A time for a call; do not resolve by narration.
- **Tony and Frank.** On [Candor] Tony knows Frank was laid off from Lordstown.
- **Laney's cry.** Her first at the table since Kaylee; Theo's "Happy birthday, Laney". The 403 call from Gordon (four minutes) is Laney's; do not have Theo know the number.
- **Carried from S2E14 and untouched:** Lena's "— and —"; Luc's gas-leak file; Theo's eyes at S2E13 s12a; the January call; the swing print and the Cabazon postcard; `promised_skye_truth`; the ring in Mémère's cedar chest; Hale's four thousand words due the Monday they return.
- **Branch-dependent:** `s2e15_dawn`, `s2e15_lourdes`, `s2e15_gift`, `s2e15_card`, `s2e15_decree`, `s2e15_page`, `s2e15_ladder`, `s2e15_jude`, `s2e15_torch`, `s2e15_val`, `s2e15_cake`, `s2e15_roses`, `s2e15_conf`, `s2e15_box`, `s2e15_d1`, `s2e15_d2`, `s2e15_tet`, `s2e15_try`, `s2e15_throw`, `s2e15_steps`.

### S2E16
> Final revision (see continuity.md S2E16). Branch tags are `[scene | condition]`. Canon is Mon Feb 15 – Tue Feb 16, 2010, to midnight. Everything said aloud in a room is heard by the people in it unless the entry says otherwise. None of the hidden canon appears on the page, in keys, cues or nextTime.

#### Jack
- **Learns from Lena (levee, one to one):** [s12a] Wes's ring photo, the ring Odile gave him "For when", his plan, and "Take care of her"; Lena learned Jack knew since Columbus Day. [s12b] the thirteen days. [s12c] Montecito, meeting Skye, the Del Playa nights, the Brick 8 night, "never that". [s12d] her apology for the song; the December card of stops; the roof; Ero cras [`lena_two_words`]; "- and you." [s12e] her Christmas decision; the January call replayed word for word [`s2e8_d3=="real"`]; she takes back "liar". [s12f] the January fights; "Do you still go up" / Facebook status [`s2e8_d3=="real"`]. [s12g] her side of the whiteboard (a doberge on her mama's cake stand). [s12i] the Julian card (index card in capitals, back steps over Christmas, in her wallet five weeks and a day, "for you", Julian reads it first); she ends it after Mass Sun Feb 21. [s12k] she gives up something "Laney's going to take personally", not coffee.
- **Learns from the others:** [s05] Remy asks if anyone else is confused; Josie: "Everybody." [s03t | Tony path] Tony asks "which one of you is with who". [s08] the rider hands Laney the coconut. [s11] Josie's marriage question at the table; Luc: the horses "come down from Canal". [s13a] Skye's midnight text: "you never said what youre wrapping."
- **Tells others:** [s12a] Becca as a guardrail; that he knew of the ring since Columbus Day. [s12b] he ended it with Becca because of her. [s12c] the meteor-shower lie [`e21_early=="lie"`]. [s12d] Magdalena was his own good-night name. [s12e] he had no "thing"; Laney took the laptop [`knows_laney_laptop && laney_silent_laptop`]. [s12g] Kevin said "leave it alone"; "So was I" [`jack_said_so_was_i && laney_aside`]. [s12h] Skye in full, without the word; he has said it to Skye in Lena's hearing [gated on the save]. [s12i] "Me too. Since the porch." [s12j] the four cigarettes he put back [Candor]; "Six." [s12k] he will tell Skye Tue Feb 23; gives up cigarettes for Lent. To Skye: [s08] the bag photo; [s10 | `s2e16_tue=="sincerity"`] "Love you" (Lena overhears); [s13a] his Lent answer (`s2e16_lent`), nothing about the levee. To Jude: [s08] his answer (`s2e16_jude`), which Lena hears. To Wes: nothing new.
- **Does not know / draws no inference:** the contents of Lena's Julian card before she tells him (s12i); that Theo and Laney will be told (they agree not to); what Lena is giving up beyond "Laney's going to take personally".

#### Lena
- **Hears on every path:** [s05] Julian's call (his name on her screen only). [s05] Josie: "Everybody." [s07] Mémère's French line, translated by Josie. [s08] the rider hands Laney the coconut. [s10] Skye's call, the Lent explanation. [s11] Josie's marriage question; Luc's "come down from Canal". [s13] Luc sees how they're standing at the sweep.
- **From Jack, one to one (levee):** [s12a] Becca as a guardrail; that he knew of the ring since Columbus Day. [s12b] the thirteen days and why Becca ended. [s12c] Montecito, meeting Skye, Del Playa, Brick 8, "never that"; the meteor-shower lie [`e21_early=="lie"`]. [s12d] Magdalena was his name for her. [s12e] he had no "thing"; Laney took the laptop [`knows_laney_laptop && laney_silent_laptop`]. [s12f] "I knelt" [`s2e8_d3=="real"`]. [s12g] the alliance named; Kevin's "leave it alone"; "So was I" [`jack_said_so_was_i && laney_aside`]. [s12h] Skye in full, without the word. [s12j] "Six."; the four put back [Candor]. [s10 | `s2e16_tue=="sincerity"`] "Love you" to Skye, overheard.

#### LIVE RISKS carried out of S2E16
- **Who knows what at the end.** Lena knows everything on the levee (Wes's plan, the thirteen days, Montecito, Skye in full, the alliance). Skye knows none of it and is to be told Tue Feb 23; Theo and Laney are not to be told. No kiss on the levee; nothing either would have to confess until Feb 23.
- **The unsaid word.** Mentioned once, in s12h ("There's a word for it" / "Don't you say it"). Whether Jack has said it to Skye in Lena's hearing depends on the save and on `s2e16_tue`. Do not have Lena say it aloud.
- **`knows_laney_laptop`.** Set on all of s12e; read it only as `knows_laney_laptop && laney_silent_laptop`. Do not have Jack know Laney took the laptop unless both hold.
- **The Julian card.** New canon: index card in capitals, back steps over Christmas, in her wallet five weeks and a day, "for you", Julian read it first; she ends it after Mass Sun Feb 21; Celeste knows he is not coming for Easter.
- **Lena's Lent.** She gives up something "Laney's going to take personally", not coffee. Laney finds out in S2E17 (nextTime). Do not reveal it earlier.
- **Jack's Lent.** Cigarettes (`lent_no_cigs`); "A pack he doesn't open"; on Candor a "Wed 2/17 · Lent" strip is in his wallet. The tin is now Lena's, in her jean-jacket pocket, and holds the "Six" strips.
- **Tin key lines.** Unconditioned in the file: correct on a 6-cigarette save only.
- **Wes.** The ring photo, the ring from Odile "For when", "Take care of her", Jack knew since Columbus Day. Lena knows all of it; the s04 Wes line reached Jack only.
- **Fork-dependent facts.** House vs Laney vs quiet (Kevin's 19th); Tony vs cake path; coat branches at Rex; `s2e8_d3=="real"` gates the replay of Hale's call, "Do you still go up" and "I knelt"; `e21_early=="lie"` gates the meteor-shower lie; `e21_reply` varies "You never wrote back"; `lena_two_words` gates Ero cras.
- **Carried from earlier and untouched:** Hale's four thousand words; Gloria's Saturday the 20th; `promised_skye_truth`; Luc's gas-leak file; Mémère's cedar-chest ring; the Cabazon postcard and the swing print.
- **Branch-dependent keys:** `s2e16_mon`, `s2e16_wes`, `s2e16_lid`, `s2e16_jude`, `s2e16_bag`, `s2e16_box`, `s2e16_candle`, `s2e16_coat`, `s2e16_tue`, `s2e16_josie`, `s2e16_kevin`, `s2e16_stop`, `s2e16_h1`, `s2e16_h2`, `s2e16_h3`, `s2e16_h5`, `s2e16_alliance`, `s2e16_liar`, `s2e16_proxy`, `s2e16_plain`, `s2e16_tin`, `s2e16_skye`, `s2e16_line`, `s2e16_ice`, `s2e16_after`, `s2e16_lent`.
